HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000675/B001","root_000956/B001","root_000956/B003","root_001212/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0228945456c7b5fbe4c2","text":"1. The closing command redirects obedience from the transgressor to humble worship. Evidence: `ط و ع B001` obedience/yielding, `ط و ع B002` mutual compliance, `س ج د B001` lowering in humble submission, `ص ل و B003` prescribed worship. Structural cue: `لا تطعه` negates the rival command before the positive pair `واسجد واقترب`. Mechanism: obedience is not abolished; it is refused in the wrong direction and enacted toward God. Abductive move: the packet supplies obedience and prostration branches; I infer that the final ayah resolves the authority conflict by reassigning compliance. Reading change: the final prohibition is constructive: disobedience to the blocker is obedience to the Lord. 2. Prostration reverses the forelock threat through voluntary lowering. Evidence: `س ج د B001` humbled lowering, `س ج د B003` prostration limbs/forehead mark, `س ج د B004` bowing/lowering head; earlier `س ف ع B001` seizing by forelock and `ن ص ي B001` forelock control. Structural cue: the head/front threatened in 96:15-16 is now lowered by command in 96:19. Mechanism: forced humiliation of the lying front contrasts with willing lowering of the worshipper. Abductive move: the packet supplies the body-part branches; I infer that the final bodily act answers the forelock scene. Reading change: prostration becomes freedom from coercive seizure, not submission to the bully. 3. `اقترب` activates devotional approach, kinship nearness, and prepared movement. Evidence: `ق ر ب B001` nearness, `ق ر ب B005` devotional approach/offering, `ق ر ب B003` kinship nearness, `ق ر ب B007` engaging/approaching a matter, `ق ر ب B008` water-seeking movement. Structural cue: after all calls and restraints, the last verb is movement toward nearness. Mechanism: refusal of false obedience opens a path of approach; the worshipper becomes a near one by an embodied act. Abductive move: the packet supplies devotional and spatial nearness; I infer that prostration is the means of approach. Reading change: the surah closes not at safety from threat but at intensified proximity. 4. The final command also activates capacity and voluntary action. Evidence: `ط و ع B003` ability/capacity, `ط و ع B004` working oneself into ability, `ط و ع B005` voluntary good beyond obligation. Structural cue: `لا تطعه` precedes two imperatives that require agency under pressure. Mechanism: refusing the opponent creates capacity for a voluntary, God-directed act. Abductive move: the packet supplies capacity/voluntary branches; I infer that the worshipper is empowered rather than merely protected. Reading change: the final ayah reads as recovered agency: do not become pliant to him; become willingly near through prostration.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000675/B001","root_001212/B001","root_001212/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1bb71ff3f32a8781df38","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:19:4\",\"root_000675\",[\"B001\"]],[\"96:19:5\",\"root_001212\",[\"B001\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Secde ile yaklaşmanın art arda gelmesi, gönüllü alçalmayı Allah'a ibadetle erişilen yakınlığın yolu hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3801af720afa93cad449","text":"No later ayah exists in this clipped focus window. The retrospective reread therefore added no later-ayah surprise beyond the already visible relations to divine sight, forelock seizure, and the paired summons in 96:14–18.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000956/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_41396e41e2e35a2625ae","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:19:3\",\"root_000956\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İtaat bütünüyle kaldırılmaz; zorlayıcı kişiden geri çekilip secde ve yaklaşma yoluyla başka bir bağlılığa yöneltilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000675/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4a1c365074f071dbff55","text":"3. **Voluntary lowering reversing forced seizure.** `س ج د:B001` supplies humble, obedient lowering, and `س ج د:B004` supplies bowing or lowering the head. The preceding scene threatens coercive seizure of a lying forelock; here the commanded subject lowers the head voluntarily. The same bodily axis carries opposite agency: forced humiliation for the controller, chosen submission for the one refusing him. I infer the reversal from adjacency. `اسجد` thus converts bodily lowering from an instrument of domination into an act of free orientation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000956/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_60dca9f6b89c7278be24","text":"2. **The practical capacity to refuse.** `ط و ع:B003` supplies ability and the conditions that make action possible. The preceding window includes divine seeing, threatened forelock seizure, and a counter-call that answers the antagonist’s council (96:14–18). Those supplied protections alter the action conditions around `لا تطعه`. I infer that the counterforce enables resistance to social coercion. The imperative therefore reads not only as obligation but as an assertion that noncompliance has been made practicable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001212/B005","root_001212/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_63e6161ff336ce486b7f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:19:5\",\"root_001212\",[\"B005\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Topluluğa yakın olmanın sağladığı ayrıcalığın karşısına, secdeyle kazanılan ilahî kabul ve erişim çıkarılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000956/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7a2ac8585c8c2a23d839","text":"1. **Refusal as a reassignment of obedience.** `ط و ع:B001` supplies pliant obedience and willing yielding; the negative imperative blocks that yielding toward `ـه`. It is immediately coordinated with two positive imperatives, `اسجد` and `اقترب`, so obedience is not abolished but redirected into another embodied relation. The packet supplies the command sequence; I infer reassignment from negative-to-positive coordination. The ayah reads as liberation from a coercive addressee through a different allegiance, not as undirected defiance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000956/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_93c4ec12b94a2a25294e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:19:3\",\"root_000956\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Önceki ilahî görüş ve karşı çağrı, itaatsizliği yalnızca görev değil, toplumsal baskı karşısında uygulanabilir bir imkân hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001212/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9add1ca5e73c376e54dd","text":"4. **Lowering as the route to nearness.** `ق ر ب:B001` supplies nearness, `ق ر ب:B005` devotional approach, and `ق ر ب:B004` favored access. The coordination `واسجد واقترب` places prostration before approach. The packet supplies sequence but does not explicitly state causation; I infer that prostration serves as the embodied means or expression of approach. `اقترب` consequently reads as gained devotional access rather than ordinary spatial movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001212/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d46e957b2a9c413b4750","text":"5. **A contest between social access and devotional access.** `ق ر ب:B004` includes privileged nearness to a ruler or inner circle, while `ق ر ب:B005` includes nearness sought through devotion. The preceding council and counterforce stage competing structures of access and protection. The two variants remain distinct: one reading hears `اقترب` as entry into favor that surpasses council-status; the other hears active devotional approach itself. I infer the contrast from the immediately preceding mobilizations. The final imperative relocates privilege from the antagonist’s social center to nearness achieved through prostration.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001212/B003","root_001212/B007","root_001212/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e22fb7385fb07049e6b5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:19:5\",\"root_001212\",[\"B003\",\"B007\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yaklaşmanın akrabalık yakınlığı ile su aramak için harekete geçme çağrışımları, son buyruğa hem aidiyet hem hayat kaynağına yönelme boyutu ekleyebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000956/B003","root_000956/B004","root_000956/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e36dbe53eac584520cdb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:19:3\",\"root_000956\",[\"B003\",\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İtaat etmeme buyruğu, kişinin gücünü zorlayıcıya teslim etmek yerine kendi isteğiyle iyiliğe yöneltmesini ve eyleme geçme yetisini yeniden kazanmasını bildirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e3a89a6b67863795463e","text":"Looking back, this ayah surprised every earlier imperative. `ٱقْرَأْ` in 96:1 and 96:3 now reads as the first form of noncompliance with false authority, while `واسجد واقترب` is the embodied completion of that reading.
